}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a strict dependency through the seed.\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    const double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict const vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict const vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict const vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t ii = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (bodies.vx[i] * bodies.vx[i] + bodies.vy[i] * bodies.vy[i] + bodies.vz[i] * bodies.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    bool nonFinite = false, posOutOfBounds = false, velOutOfBounds = false;\n\n    // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n    const double maxPos = 1e6;\n    const double maxVel = 1e6;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : nonFinite, posOutOfBounds, velOutOfBounds)\n    for (size_t i = 0; }\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a strict dependency through the seed.\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force kernel body; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(bodies.px[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.py[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.pz[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(bodies.vx[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vz[i])) {\n            nonFinite = true;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (std::abs(bodies.px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n            posOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(bodies.vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n            velOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report in the same priority order as the original sequential scan.\n    if (nonFinite) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (posOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (velOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// libgomp leaves the team unbound unless OMP_PROC_BIND/OMP_PLACES are set, and\n// letting the scheduler migrate threads costs several x at high thread counts.\n// Spread the team over the CPUs this process is already allowed to run on, one\n// thread per CPU, unless the user configured binding explicitly.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || omp_get_num_places() > 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[omp_get_thread_num() % cpus.size()], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    BodySystem bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    // Pin the team before timing so the threads do not migrate between cores\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole simulation: the per-step work shares\n    // the same team and only pays for the implicit barriers between phases.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(bodies);\n        integrateBodies(bodies);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.px[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.py[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.pz[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vx[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vy[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vz[i]);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);\n            printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":7,"oldLines":6,"newStart":7,"newLines":7,"lines":[" #include <vector>"," "," #include <omp.h>","+#include <sched.h>"," "," #include \"../common/results_output.hpp\""," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

}\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a strict dependency through the seed.\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force kernel body; ii < n; ii += TILE) {\n        const size_t m = std::min(TILE, n - ii);\n\n        double ix[TILE], iy[TILE], iz[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < m; Each lane\n// keeps its own force accumulator and walks j in ascending order, so the\n// floating-point summation order per body is identical to the original scalar\n// implementation. The wide tile amortizes the sqrt/divide latency better; the\n// narrow one is used when the body count is too small to keep every thread\n// busy otherwise.\nconstexpr size_t WIDE_TILE = 8;\nconstexpr size_t NARROW_TILE = 4;\n\n// Structure-of-arrays body storage: contiguous coordinate streams let the\n// inner j-loop broadcast a single body and the i-tile load with plain\n// vector loads instead of gathers.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz;\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t n) : px(n), py(n), pz(n), vx(n), vy(n), vz(n) {}\n\n    size_t size() const { return px.size(); }\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a strict dependency through the seed.\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force kernel body; called from inside an already-open parallel region.\ntemplate <size_t TILE>\nvoid computeForcesTiled(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    const double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict const vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict const vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict const vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t ii = 0; ii < n; ii += TILE) {\n        const size_t m = std::min(TILE, n - ii);\n\n        double ix[TILE], iy[TILE], iz[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            ix[k] = px[ii + k];\n            iy[k] = py[ii + k];\n            iz[k] = pz[ii + k];\n            Fx[k] = 0.0;\n            Fy[k] = 0.0;\n            Fz[k] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double jx = px[j], jy = py[j], jz = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd simdlen(TILE)\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n                const double dx = jx - ix[k];\n                const double dy = jy - iy[k];\n                const double dz = jz - iz[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            vx[ii + k] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vy[ii + k] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vz[ii + k] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatches to the tile width picked for this problem size. The predicate is\n// uniform across the team, so every thread enters the same worksharing loop.\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies, const bool wide) {\n    if (wide) {\n        computeForcesTiled<WIDE_TILE>(bodies);\n    } else {\n        computeForcesTiled<NARROW_TILE>(bodies);\n    }\n}\n\n// Position update; called from inside an already-open parallel region.\nvoid integrateBodies(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict const vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict const vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict const vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp for simd schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    const double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (bodies.vx[i] * bodies.vx[i] + bodies.vy[i] * bodies.vy[i] + bodies.vz[i] * bodies.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). Each i contributes\n    // its own partial sum; the partials are folded together in ascending i\n    // order so the result is reproducible regardless of the thread count.\n    std::vector<double> partial(n, 0.0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local += 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        partial[i] = local;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    bool nonFinite = false, posOutOfBounds = false, velOutOfBounds = false;\n\n    // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n    const double maxPos = 1e6;\n    const double maxVel = 1e6;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : nonFinite, posOutOfBounds, velOutOfBounds)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(bodies.px[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.py[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.pz[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(bodies.vx[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vz[i])) {\n            nonFinite = true;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (std::abs(bodies.px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n            posOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(bodies.vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n            velOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report in the same priority order as the original sequential scan.\n    if (nonFinite) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (posOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (velOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// libgomp leaves the team unbound unless OMP_PROC_BIND/OMP_PLACES are set, and\n// letting the scheduler migrate threads costs several x at high thread counts.\n// Spread the team over the CPUs this process is already allowed to run on, one\n// thread per CPU, unless the user configured binding explicitly.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || omp_get_num_places() > 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[omp_get_thread_num() % cpus.size()], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    BodySystem bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    // Pin the team before timing so the threads do not migrate between cores\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole simulation: the per-step work shares\n    // the same team and only pays for the implicit barriers between phases.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(bodies);\n        integrateBodies(bodies);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.px[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.py[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.pz[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vx[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vy[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vz[i]);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);\n            printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":297,"oldLines":9,"newStart":297,"newLines":11,"lines":[" ","     // One parallel region for the whole simulation: the per-step work shares","     // the same team and only pays for the implicit barriers between phases.","+    const bool wideTile = static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) >= WIDE_TILE * omp_get_max_threads();","+"," #pragma omp parallel","     for (int step = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local += 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        partial[i] = local;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    bool nonFinite = false, posOutOfBounds = false, velOutOfBounds = false;\n\n    // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n    const double maxPos = 1e6;\n    const double maxVel = 1e6;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : nonFinite, posOutOfBounds, velOutOfBounds)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(bodies.px[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.py[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.pz[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(bodies.vx[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vz[i])) {\n            nonFinite = true;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (std::abs(bodies.px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n            posOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(bodies.vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n            velOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report in the same priority order as the original sequential scan.\n    if (nonFinite) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (posOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (velOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// libgomp leaves the team unbound unless OMP_PROC_BIND/OMP_PLACES are set, and\n// letting the scheduler migrate threads costs several x at high thread counts.\n// Spread the team over the CPUs this process is already allowed to run on, one\n// thread per CPU, unless the user configured binding explicitly.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || omp_get_num_places() > 0) {\n        return;","numLines":120,"startLine":100,"totalLines":347}}}

Each lane\n// keeps its own force accumulator and walks j in ascending order, so the\n// floating-point summation order per body is identical to the original scalar\n// implementation. The wide tile amortizes the sqrt/divide latency better; the\n// narrow one is used when the body count is too small to keep every thread\n// busy otherwise.\nconstexpr size_t WIDE_TILE = 8;\nconstexpr size_t NARROW_TILE = 4;\n\n// Structure-of-arrays body storage: contiguous coordinate streams let the\n// inner j-loop broadcast a single body and the i-tile load with plain\n// vector loads instead of gathers.\nstruct BodySystem {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz;\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz;\n\n    explicit BodySystem(const size_t n) : px(n), py(n), pz(n), vx(n), vy(n), vz(n) {}\n\n    size_t size() const { return px.size(); }\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(BodySystem& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    // Kept sequential: rand_r carries a strict dependency through the seed.\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies.px[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.py[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.pz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vx[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vy[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        bodies.vz[i] = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Force kernel body; called from inside an already-open parallel region.\ntemplate <size_t TILE>\nvoid computeForcesTiled(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    const double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict const vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict const vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict const vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t ii = 0; ii < n; ii += TILE) {\n        const size_t m = std::min(TILE, n - ii);\n\n        double ix[TILE], iy[TILE], iz[TILE];\n        double Fx[TILE], Fy[TILE], Fz[TILE];\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            ix[k] = px[ii + k];\n            iy[k] = py[ii + k];\n            iz[k] = pz[ii + k];\n            Fx[k] = 0.0;\n            Fy[k] = 0.0;\n            Fz[k] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double jx = px[j], jy = py[j], jz = pz[j];\n\n#pragma omp simd simdlen(TILE)\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n                const double dx = jx - ix[k];\n                const double dy = jy - iy[k];\n                const double dz = jz - iz[k];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < m; ++k) {\n            vx[ii + k] += DT * Fx[k];\n            vy[ii + k] += DT * Fy[k];\n            vz[ii + k] += DT * Fz[k];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Dispatches to the tile width picked for this problem size. The predicate is\n// uniform across the team, so every thread enters the same worksharing loop.\nvoid computeForces(BodySystem& bodies, const bool wide) {\n    if (wide) {\n        computeForcesTiled<WIDE_TILE>(bodies);\n    } else {\n        computeForcesTiled<NARROW_TILE>(bodies);\n    }\n}\n\n// Position update; called from inside an already-open parallel region.\nvoid integrateBodies(BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n    const double* __restrict const vx = bodies.vx.data();\n    const double* __restrict const vy = bodies.vy.data();\n    const double* __restrict const vz = bodies.vz.data();\n\n#pragma omp for simd schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        px[i] += vx[i] * DT;\n        py[i] += vy[i] * DT;\n        pz[i] += vz[i] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    const double* __restrict const px = bodies.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict const py = bodies.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict const pz = bodies.pz.data();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (bodies.vx[i] * bodies.vx[i] + bodies.vy[i] * bodies.vy[i] + bodies.vz[i] * bodies.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies). Each i contributes\n    // its own partial sum; the partials are folded together in ascending i\n    // order so the result is reproducible regardless of the thread count.\n    std::vector<double> partial(n, 0.0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic, 16)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double local = 0.0;\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - px[i];\n            const double dy = py[j] - py[i];\n            const double dz = pz[j] - pz[i];\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            local += 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        partial[i] = local;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        energy -= partial[i];\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const BodySystem& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    bool nonFinite = false, posOutOfBounds = false, velOutOfBounds = false;\n\n    // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n    const double maxPos = 1e6;\n    const double maxVel = 1e6;\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : nonFinite, posOutOfBounds, velOutOfBounds)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(bodies.px[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.py[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.pz[i]) ||\n            !std::isfinite(bodies.vx[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vy[i]) || !std::isfinite(bodies.vz[i])) {\n            nonFinite = true;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        if (std::abs(bodies.px[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.py[i]) > maxPos || std::abs(bodies.pz[i]) > maxPos) {\n            posOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(bodies.vx[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vy[i]) > maxVel || std::abs(bodies.vz[i]) > maxVel) {\n            velOutOfBounds = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report in the same priority order as the original sequential scan.\n    if (nonFinite) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (posOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (velOutOfBounds) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// libgomp leaves the team unbound unless OMP_PROC_BIND/OMP_PLACES are set, and\n// letting the scheduler migrate threads costs several x at high thread counts.\n// Spread the team over the CPUs this process is already allowed to run on, one\n// thread per CPU, unless the user configured binding explicitly.\nvoid bindThreads() {\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") != nullptr || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\") != nullptr || omp_get_num_places() > 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) {\n            cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        cpu_set_t self;\n        CPU_ZERO(&self);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[omp_get_thread_num() % cpus.size()], &self);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(self), &self);\n    }\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Initialize bodies\n    BodySystem bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    // Pin the team before timing so the threads do not migrate between cores\n    bindThreads();\n\n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole simulation: the per-step work shares\n    // the same team and only pays for the implicit barriers between phases.\n    const bool wideTile = static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) >= WIDE_TILE * omp_get_max_threads();\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(bodies, wideTile);\n        integrateBodies(bodies);\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.px[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.py[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.pz[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vx[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vy[i]);\n            bodyData.push_back(bodies.vz[i]);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n Each lane owns its force accumulator and walks `j` in ascending order, so **the per-body summation order is unchanged from the original** — results are bit-identical across any thread count, and vectorization comes from doing several `i` at once rather than from reassociating the reduction.\n- Tile width 8, dropping to 4 when `n < 8 × max_threads` so small problems still fill the machine (identical results either way).\n- **One parallel region for the entire step loop** (`computeForces`/`integrateBodies` are worksharing bodies with implicit barriers), so per-step team startup is gone.\n- **Thread pinning** at startup: libgomp leaves threads unbound by default, and migration cost 4x at 256 threads on this box.
